[briefing | 1. The room at rest | heading 2] Three watched accounts have public, dated hiring activity
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Boeing is rebuilding its production workforce after the strike, Chipotle has a recurring seasonal hiring program of roughly 20,000 spring hires, and CoreWeave's datacenter expansion has been accompanied by rapid headcount growth. These public events provide current, evidence-based reasons to evaluate whether the accounts have hiring problems relevant to your product.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] WHAT CHANGED
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Three watched accounts have public, dated hiring events.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] EVIDENCE
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Company announcements, filings, and news coverage for the three accounts.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] WHY THIS CONFIDENCE
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] The underlying events are supported by public sources, including company announcements.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] WHY IT MATTERS
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] A public hiring event provides a timely reason to research the account and can support relevant outreach when the hiring need matches your ICP.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] WHO NEEDS TO KNOW
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] The seller responsible for Boeing, Chipotle, and CoreWeave outreach.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] WHAT NEXT
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Reference the account's own announcement and connect it to the relevant hiring-volume problem.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] RECOMMENDED MOVES
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Draft outreach tied to each hiring event
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] The relevance of a public announcement declines as it becomes older.

[briefing | 1. The room at rest | heading 2] Recorded food-service deals have moved faster than recorded airline deals
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] The current workspace shows Chipotle closing in 132 days, while the recorded airline deals have taken longer on average and often identify the final approver later in the cycle. The territory tiers currently prioritize aviation. The recorded sample is limited, but it is large enough to justify reviewing whether the current territory priority still matches your own deal history.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] A cycle-length difference is visible between the recorded food-service and airline deals.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Closed-deal history compared with the current territory tiers.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] The observation comes from the workspace's own deal history; the sample size is still limited.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Territory tiers determine where seller time is concentrated, so a persistent difference in recorded performance should be reviewed.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] The person responsible for territory prioritization.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Review the territory tiers and either change the priority or record why aviation should remain first.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Review territory tiers against closed-deal performance The current tiers and recorded outcomes point in different directions. Territory Architect · tiers · review

[briefing | 1. The room at rest | heading 2] Automated-hiring compliance requirements are appearing in enterprise security reviews
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] New York's automated-employment law requires bias-audit-related controls, and similar language can appear in enterprise security questionnaires. Boeing's current review includes audit-related questions. The recorded Palantir loss also involved a late security review, which makes early compliance preparation relevant, although that single loss does not establish that security timing alone caused the outcome.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Hiring-AI compliance language is present in an active security questionnaire.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Public New York regulation plus the recorded Boeing security questionnaire and Palantir deal history.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] The regulatory requirement and current questionnaire are directly supported. The effect of security timing on deal outcomes is an assessment based on limited internal history.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Known compliance requirements can create review work and delay if they are discovered late in the buying process.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] The person responsible for security review on active deals.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Address known automated-hiring compliance requirements early on deals that require security review.
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] Review the current security workstream
[briefing | 1. The room at rest | text] An unresolved security issue can delay the expected close date.

[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Data controls
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Download or restore selected cloud records, manage this browser's copy, or delete the records listed below.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Download workspace export
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Downloads the records listed below as JSON. Any read errors are recorded in the file. This is a partial workspace export.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | button] Download workspace export
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Which records are included?
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Export and delete include ICPs, deals, proofs, network requests, watched accounts, sequences, discovery call logs, saved studio content, pipeline settings, discovery frameworks, readiness snapshots and sales handoff documents.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Export also includes events and the workspace's commercial profile. Delete retains those records. Neither control covers all workspace content: contacts, activity history, Briefing items, monitoring rules and other records can remain outside this list.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Each operation processes up to 5,000 records per included category, 100 pipeline settings and one commercial profile. Records beyond these limits are not exported or deleted. Your account, workspace and membership are retained.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Restore from an export
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Restores the rows present in a prior Antaeus cloud export. Table-level restore errors are reported if any write fails.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Restore from a file
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Import closed-deal history
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Paste historical closed deals from a spreadsheet, one deal per line: account, deal size, outcome, close date, and loss reason where applicable. A header row is allowed. Historical deals can improve ICP, win/loss, and planning analysis before new deals close in Antaeus.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | field] account,value,outcome,date,reason Northwind,"$80,000",won,2026-03-04, Apex Mfg,64000,lost,2026-04-18,went with a competitor
[settings | 1. The room at rest | button] Add deals
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Device copy
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Some workflows keep a local browser copy. Exporting or clearing this device copy does not delete the cloud records covered by the cloud workspace controls above.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | button] Export device copy
[settings | 1. The room at rest | button] Clear device copy

[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Deletes the cloud records listed above within the stated limits and clears this browser's Antaeus copy. Other cloud records remain. Download an export first if you may need the included data later.

[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Automatic activity capture
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Optional capture methods can reduce manual logging. Review what each method records before enabling it.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Email BCC capture
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Add your Antaeus capture address to BCC on outreach messages. The capture workflow attempts to match the send to a watched account and log the activity. The current capture flow stores message metadata rather than the message body.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] BCC capture is not configured in this environment yet.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] How to use it
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Add the capture address to the BCC field when you send outreach.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] If your email or sales tool supports a persistent BCC setting, you can add the address there instead of entering it on every message.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Calendar capture
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Connect an ICS calendar URL to check for meetings that match watched accounts. The sync reports how many meetings matched in the current window.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Calendar URL
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Use a calendar subscription URL that you are comfortable connecting to Antaeus. Follow your calendar provider's instructions for generating the appropriate ICS link.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | field] https://…/calendar.ics

[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Direct inbox connection
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] A direct read-only inbox connection is not available in this workflow today. Use BCC capture above when you want email activity captured automatically.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Calls with your existing tools
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Antaeus does not include a dialer. Use your existing calling tool and record the outcome through the relevant Antaeus workflow.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Cold calls: review the plan in Cold Call Studio before dialing, then record the outcome after the call. A booked meeting creates a deal automatically.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] Scheduled discovery calls: use Discovery Studio during the call when you want its questions, notes, and deal fields available.
[settings | 1. The room at rest | text] If your calling tool creates a recording or transcript, review it and add the relevant buyer information and dated next step to the Antaeus workflow you used.
